## Zabura 1:1 (id: 10019001001)

1Mai farin ciki ne mutumin da ba ya aiki da shawarar mugaye ko yă yi abin da masu zunubi suke yi ko yă zauna tare da masu yin ba’a.


## Zabura 1:2 (id: 10019001002)

2Amma yana jin daɗin bin dokar Ubangiji, kuma yana ta nazarinta dare da rana.


## Zabura 1:3 (id: 10019001003)

3Yana kama da itacen da aka shuka kusa da maɓulɓulan ruwa, wanda yake ba da amfani a daidai lokaci wanda kuma ganyensa ba sa bushewa. Duk abin da yake yi, yakan yi nasara.


## Zabura 1:4 (id: 10019001004)

## Zabura 1:5 (id: 10019001005)

## Zabura 1:6 (id: 10019001006)

## Zabura 2:12 (id: 10019002012)

## Zabura 3:0 (id: 10019003000)

0Zabura ta Dawuda. Sa’ad da ya gudu daga ɗansa Absalom.


## Zabura 3:1 (id: 10019003001)

1Ya Ubangiji, abokan gābana nawa ne! Su nawa suke gāba da ni!


## Zabura 3:2 (id: 10019003002)

## Zabura 3:3 (id: 10019003003)

## Zabura 3:4 (id: 10019003004)

## Zabura 4:0 (id: 10019004000)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Da kayan kiɗi masu tsirkiya. Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 4:1 (id: 10019004001)

1Ka amsa mini sa’ad da na kira gare ka, Ya Allahna mai adalci. Ka ba ni sauƙi daga wahalata; ka ji tausayina ka kuma ji addu’ata.


## Zabura 4:2 (id: 10019004002)

2Har yaushe, ya ku mutane za ku mai da ɗaukakata abin kunya? Har yaushe za ku ƙaunaci ruɗu ku nemi allolin ƙarya? Sela


## Zabura 4:3 (id: 10019004003)

## Zabura 4:4 (id: 10019004004)

## Zabura 4:6 (id: 10019004006)

## Zabura 4:7 (id: 10019004007)

## Zabura 4:8 (id: 10019004008)

## Zabura 5:0 (id: 10019005000)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Don Busa. Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 5:1 (id: 10019005001)

1Ka saurare kalmomina, ya Ubangiji, ka ji ajiyar zuciyata.


## Zabura 5:2 (id: 10019005002)

2Ka saurare kukata na neman taimako, Sarkina da Allahna, gama gare ka nake addu’a.


## Zabura 5:3 (id: 10019005003)

3Da safe, ya Ubangiji, ka ji muryata; da safe na gabatar da bukatuna a gabanka in kuma jira da sa zuciya.


## Zabura 5:4 (id: 10019005004)

4Kai ba Allahn da yake jin daɗin mugu ba ne; gama kana gāba da dukan masu aikata mugunta.


## Zabura 5:5 (id: 10019005005)

5Masu fariya ba za su iya tsaya a gabanka ba. Kana ƙin duk masu aikata abin da ba daidai ba.


## Zabura 5:6 (id: 10019005006)

6Kakan hallaka masu yin ƙarya. Masu kisankai da mutane masu ruɗu Ubangiji yana ƙyama.


## Zabura 5:7 (id: 10019005007)

7Amma ni, ta wurin jinƙanka mai girma, zan shiga gidanka; da bangirma zan durƙusa ga haikalinka.


## Zabura 5:8 (id: 10019005008)

8Ka bi da ni, ya Ubangiji, cikin adalcinka saboda abokan gābana, ka fayyace hanyarka a gabana.


## Zabura 5:9 (id: 10019005009)

9Babu ko kalma guda daga bakinsu da za a dogara da ita; marmarinsu kawai shi ne su hallakar da waɗansu. Maƙogwaronsu buɗaɗɗen kabari ne; da harshensu suna faɗin ƙarya.


## Zabura 5:10 (id: 10019005010)

10Ka furta su masu laifi, ya Allah! Bari makircinsu yă zama fāɗuwarsu. Ka kore su saboda yawan zunubansu, saboda sun tayar maka.


## Zabura 5:11 (id: 10019005011)

11Amma bari dukan waɗanda suke neman mafaka gare ka su yi murna; bari kullum su yi ta rerawa saboda farin ciki. Ka buɗe kāriyarka a bisansu, domin waɗanda suke ƙaunar sunanka su yi farin ciki a cikinka.


## Zabura 5:12 (id: 10019005012)

12Gama tabbatacce, ya Ubangiji, kakan albarkaci adalai; kakan kewaye su da tagomashinka kamar garkuwa.


## Zabura 6:0 (id: 10019006000)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Da kayan kiɗi masu tsirkiya. Bisa ga sheminit. Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 6:1 (id: 10019006001)

1Ya Ubangiji, kada ka tsawata mini cikin fushinka ko ka hore ni cikin hasalarka.


## Zabura 6:2 (id: 10019006002)

## Zabura 6:3 (id: 10019006003)

3Raina yana cikin wahala. Har yaushe, ya Ubangiji, har yaushe?


## Zabura 6:4 (id: 10019006004)

## Zabura 6:5 (id: 10019006005)

## Zabura 6:6 (id: 10019006006)

## Zabura 6:7 (id: 10019006007)

## Zabura 6:8 (id: 10019006008)

## Zabura 6:9 (id: 10019006009)

## Zabura 6:10 (id: 10019006010)

10Dukan abokan gābana za su sha kunya su kuma karaya; za su juya da kunya nan da nan.


## Zabura 7:0 (id: 10019007000)

0Wani shiggayiyon ta Dawuda, wanda Ya Rera wa Ubangiji game da Kush mutumin Benyamin.


## Zabura 7:1 (id: 10019007001)

1Ya Ubangiji Allahna, na zo neman mafaka a wurinka; ka cece ni ka kuɓutar da ni daga dukan waɗanda suke bi na,


## Zabura 7:2 (id: 10019007002)

2in ba haka ba za su yayyage ni kamar zaki su ɓarke ni kucu\-kucu ba tare da wani da zai cece ni ba.


## Zabura 7:3 (id: 10019007003)

3Ya Ubangiji Allahna, in na yi kuskure aka kuwa sami laifi a hannuwana,


## Zabura 7:4 (id: 10019007004)

## Zabura 7:5 (id: 10019007005)

5to, bari abokin gābana yă bi yă kuma cim mini; bari yă tattake raina a ƙasa ya sa in kwana a ƙura. Sela


## Zabura 7:6 (id: 10019007006)

## Zabura 7:7 (id: 10019007007)

7Bari taron mutane su taru kewaye da kai. Ka yi mulki a bisansu daga bisa;


## Zabura 7:8 (id: 10019007008)

8bari Ubangiji mai shari’ar mutane. Ka shari’anta ni Ya Ubangiji, bisa ga adalcina, bisa ga mutuncina, ya Mafi Ɗaukaka.


## Zabura 7:9 (id: 10019007009)

9Ya Allah mai adalci, wanda yake binciken tunani da zukata, ka kawo ƙarshen rikicin mugaye ka kuma sa adalai su zauna lafiya.


## Zabura 7:10 (id: 10019007010)

10Garkuwata shi ne Allah Mafi Ɗaukaka, wanda yake ceton masu tsabtar zuciya.


## Zabura 7:11 (id: 10019007011)

## Zabura 7:12 (id: 10019007012)

## Zabura 7:13 (id: 10019007013)

## Zabura 7:14 (id: 10019007014)

14Wanda yake da cikin mugunta ya kuma ɗauki cikin damuwa yakan haifi ƙarya.


## Zabura 7:15 (id: 10019007015)

15Wanda ya haƙa rami yakan fāɗa cikin ramin da ya haƙa.


## Zabura 7:16 (id: 10019007016)

## Zabura 7:17 (id: 10019007017)

## Zabura 8:0 (id: 10019008000)

## Zabura 8:7 (id: 10019008007)

## Zabura 8:8 (id: 10019008008)

## Zabura 8:9 (id: 10019008009)

## Zabura 9:0 (id: 10019009000)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Ga muryar “Mutuwar Ɗa.” Zabura ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 9:1 (id: 10019009001)

## Zabura 9:2 (id: 10019009002)

## Zabura 9:3 (id: 10019009003)

## Zabura 9:4 (id: 10019009004)

## Zabura 9:5 (id: 10019009005)

## Zabura 9:6 (id: 10019009006)

## Zabura 9:7 (id: 10019009007)

## Zabura 9:8 (id: 10019009008)

## Zabura 9:9 (id: 10019009009)

## Zabura 9:10 (id: 10019009010)

## Zabura 9:11 (id: 10019009011)

## Zabura 9:12 (id: 10019009012)

## Zabura 9:13 (id: 10019009013)

## Zabura 9:14 (id: 10019009014)

14don in furta yabanka cikin ƙofofin ’Yar Sihiyona a can kuwa in yi farin ciki cikin cetonka.


## Zabura 9:15 (id: 10019009015)

15Al’umma sun fāɗa cikin ramin da suka haƙa wa waɗansu; aka kama ƙafafunsu a ragar da suka ɓoye.


## Zabura 9:16 (id: 10019009016)

## Zabura 9:18 (id: 10019009018)

## Zabura 9:19 (id: 10019009019)

## Zabura 9:20 (id: 10019009020)

## Zabura 10:1 (id: 10019010001)

1Don me, ya Ubangiji, kake tsaya can da nesa? Me ya sa kake ɓoye kanka a lokutan wahala?


## Zabura 10:2 (id: 10019010002)

## Zabura 10:3 (id: 10019010003)

## Zabura 10:4 (id: 10019010004)

## Zabura 10:12 (id: 10019010012)

## Zabura 10:13 (id: 10019010013)

## Zabura 10:15 (id: 10019010015)

## Zabura 10:17 (id: 10019010017)

## Zabura 10:18 (id: 10019010018)

## Zabura 11:0 (id: 10019011000)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Don busa. Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 11:1 (id: 10019011001)

## Zabura 11:7 (id: 10019011007)

0Ta Dawuda.

## Zabura 25:5 (id: 10019025005)

## Zabura 25:6 (id: 10019025006)

## Zabura 25:8 (id: 10019025008)

## Zabura 25:9 (id: 10019025009)

## Zabura 25:10 (id: 10019025010)

## Zabura 25:11 (id: 10019025011)

## Zabura 25:12 (id: 10019025012)

## Zabura 25:13 (id: 10019025013)

## Zabura 25:14 (id: 10019025014)

## Zabura 25:16 (id: 10019025016)

## Zabura 25:17 (id: 10019025017)

## Zabura 25:20 (id: 10019025020)

## Zabura 25:22 (id: 10019025022)

0Ta Dawuda.

## Zabura 26:6 (id: 10019026006)

## Zabura 26:7 (id: 10019026007)

## Zabura 26:8 (id: 10019026008)

## Zabura 26:9 (id: 10019026009)

## Zabura 26:10 (id: 10019026010)

## Zabura 26:11 (id: 10019026011)

## Zabura 27:0 (id: 10019027000)

0Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 27:1 (id: 10019027001)

1Ubangiji ne haskena da cetona, wa zan ji tsoro? Ubangiji ne mafakar raina, wane ne zan ji tsoro?


## Zabura 27:2 (id: 10019027002)

2Sa’ad da mugaye suka tasar mini don su cinye ni, sa’ad da abokan gābana da maƙiyina suka kawo mini hari, sun yi tuntuɓe suka fāɗi.


## Zabura 27:3 (id: 10019027003)

3Ko da yake mayaƙa sun kewaye ni, zuciyata ba za tă ji tsoro ba; ko da yake ya ɓarke a kaina, duk da haka zan ƙarfafa.


## Zabura 27:4 (id: 10019027004)

4Abu guda na roƙi Ubangiji, wannan shi ne na nema, cewa in zauna a gidan Ubangiji dukan kwanakin raina, in dubi kyan Ubangiji in kuma neme shi a cikin haikalinsa.


## Zabura 27:5 (id: 10019027005)

5Gama a lokacin wahala, zai kiyaye ni lafiya a wurin zamansa; zai ɓoye ni cikin inuwar tabanakul nasa ya kuma sa ni can a bisa dutse.


## Zabura 27:6 (id: 10019027006)

## Zabura 27:7 (id: 10019027007)

7Ka ji muryata sa’ad da na yi kira, ya Ubangiji; ka yi mini jinƙai ka kuma amsa mini.


## Zabura 27:8 (id: 10019027008)

## Zabura 27:9 (id: 10019027009)

9Kada ka ɓoye fuskarka daga gare ni, kada ka kore bawanka daga gare ka cikin fushi; kai ne ka kasance mai taimakona. Kada ka ƙi ni ko ka yashe ni, Ya Allah Mai cetona.


## Zabura 27:10 (id: 10019027010)

10Ko da mahaifina da mahaifiyata sun yashe ni, Ubangiji zai karɓe ni.


## Zabura 27:11 (id: 10019027011)

11Ka koya mini hanyarka, ya Ubangiji; ka bi da ni a miƙaƙƙiyar hanya saboda masu danne ni.


## Zabura 27:12 (id: 10019027012)

12Kada ka ba da ni da sha’awar maƙiyina, gama masu shaidar ƙarya sun taso a kaina, suna numfasa tashin hankali.


## Zabura 27:13 (id: 10019027013)

13Har yanzu ina da ƙarfin gwiwa a wannan cewa zan ga alherin Ubangiji; a ƙasar masu rai.


## Zabura 27:14 (id: 10019027014)

14Ka dogara ga Ubangiji, ka yi ƙarfin hali ka yi ƙarfin zuciya ka kuma dogara ga Ubangiji.


## Zabura 28:0 (id: 10019028000)

0Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 28:1 (id: 10019028001)

## Zabura 28:6 (id: 10019028006)

## Zabura 28:7 (id: 10019028007)

## Zabura 28:8 (id: 10019028008)

## Zabura 28:9 (id: 10019028009)

## Zabura 29:0 (id: 10019029000)

0Zabura ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 29:1 (id: 10019029001)

## Zabura 29:5 (id: 10019029005)

## Zabura 29:6 (id: 10019029006)

## Zabura 29:7 (id: 10019029007)

## Zabura 29:8 (id: 10019029008)

8Muryar Ubangiji ta girgiza hamada Ubangiji ya girgiza Hamadan Kadesh.


## Zabura 29:9 (id: 10019029009)

## Zabura 29:10 (id: 10019029010)

## Zabura 29:11 (id: 10019029011)

## Zabura 30:3 (id: 10019030003)

## Zabura 30:7 (id: 10019030007)

## Zabura 33:22 (id: 10019033022)

## Zabura 34:0 (id: 10019034000)

0Ta Dawuda. Sa’ad da ya yi kamar ya haukace a gaban Abimelek, Wanda ya kore shi, ya kuwa tafi.


## Zabura 34:1 (id: 10019034001)

1Zan gode wa Ubangiji kullayaumi; yabonsa kullum za su kasance a leɓunana.


## Zabura 34:2 (id: 10019034002)

## Zabura 34:3 (id: 10019034003)

3Ku ɗaukaka Ubangiji tare da ni; bari mu ɗaukaka sunansa tare.


## Zabura 34:4 (id: 10019034004)

4Na nemi Ubangiji, ya kuwa amsa mini; ya cece ni daga dukan tsoro.


## Zabura 34:5 (id: 10019034005)

## Zabura 34:6 (id: 10019034006)

## Zabura 34:7 (id: 10019034007)

## Zabura 34:8 (id: 10019034008)

## Zabura 34:9 (id: 10019034009)

9Ku ji tsoron Ubangiji, ku tsarkakansa, gama masu tsoronsa ba sa rasa kome.


## Zabura 34:10 (id: 10019034010)

10Zakoki za su iya rasa ƙarfi su kuma ji yunwa, amma waɗanda suke neman Ubangiji ba sa rasa abu mai kyau.


## Zabura 34:11 (id: 10019034011)

11Ku zo, ’ya’yana, ku saurare ni; zan koya muku tsoron Ubangiji.


## Zabura 34:12 (id: 10019034012)

12Duk waninku da yake ƙaunar rayuwa yana kuma so yă ga kwanaki masu kyau,


## Zabura 34:13 (id: 10019034013)

## Zabura 34:14 (id: 10019034014)

## Zabura 34:15 (id: 10019034015)

## Zabura 34:16 (id: 10019034016)

## Zabura 34:17 (id: 10019034017)

## Zabura 34:18 (id: 10019034018)

18Ubangiji yana kusa da waɗanda suka karai ya kuma cece waɗanda aka ragargaza a ruhu.


## Zabura 34:19 (id: 10019034019)

## Zabura 34:20 (id: 10019034020)

## Zabura 34:21 (id: 10019034021)

## Zabura 34:22 (id: 10019034022)

22Ubangiji yakan cece bayinsa; ba wanda yake neman mafaka a wurinsa da za a hukunta.

0Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 35:1 (id: 10019035001)

## Zabura 35:8 (id: 10019035008)

## Zabura 35:10 (id: 10019035010)

## Zabura 35:11 (id: 10019035011)

## Zabura 35:12 (id: 10019035012)

## Zabura 35:13 (id: 10019035013)

## Zabura 35:14 (id: 10019035014)

## Zabura 35:15 (id: 10019035015)

## Zabura 35:17 (id: 10019035017)

## Zabura 35:18 (id: 10019035018)

## Zabura 35:19 (id: 10019035019)

## Zabura 35:20 (id: 10019035020)

## Zabura 35:22 (id: 10019035022)

## Zabura 35:24 (id: 10019035024)

## Zabura 35:25 (id: 10019035025)

## Zabura 35:27 (id: 10019035027)

## Zabura 35:28 (id: 10019035028)

## Zabura 36:0 (id: 10019036000)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Ta Dawuda bawan Ubangiji.


## Zabura 36:1 (id: 10019036001)

1Akwai magana a cikin zuciyata game da zunubin mugu. Babu tsoron Allah a idanunsa.


## Zabura 36:2 (id: 10019036002)

2Gama a idanunsa yana ganin kansa wani abu ne wanda ya sha ƙarfi a gane ko ya ƙi zunubinsa.


## Zabura 36:3 (id: 10019036003)

3Kalmomin bakinsa mugaye ne da kuma ruɗu; ya daina zama mai hikima da yin alheri.


## Zabura 36:4 (id: 10019036004)

## Zabura 36:5 (id: 10019036005)

## Zabura 36:6 (id: 10019036006)

## Zabura 36:7 (id: 10019036007)

## Zabura 36:8 (id: 10019036008)

## Zabura 36:9 (id: 10019036009)

## Zabura 36:10 (id: 10019036010)

## Zabura 36:11 (id: 10019036011)

11Kada ka bari ƙafar mai girman kai yă fāɗa mini, ko hannun mugu ya kore ni.


## Zabura 36:12 (id: 10019036012)

12Dubi yadda masu aikata zunubi suka fāɗi, a zube, ba za su kuwa iya tashi ba!


## Zabura 37:0 (id: 10019037000)

0Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 37:1 (id: 10019037001)

## Zabura 37:3 (id: 10019037003)

## Zabura 37:4 (id: 10019037004)

## Zabura 37:5 (id: 10019037005)

## Zabura 37:6 (id: 10019037006)

## Zabura 37:7 (id: 10019037007)

## Zabura 86:0 (id: 10019086000)

0Addu’ar Dawuda.


## Zabura 86:1 (id: 10019086001)

## Zabura 86:2 (id: 10019086002)

## Zabura 86:3 (id: 10019086003)

## Zabura 86:5 (id: 10019086005)

## Zabura 86:6 (id: 10019086006)

6Ka ji addu’ata, ya Ubangiji; ka saurari kukata na neman jinƙai.


## Zabura 86:7 (id: 10019086007)

## Zabura 86:8 (id: 10019086008)

## Zabura 86:9 (id: 10019086009)

## Zabura 86:10 (id: 10019086010)

## Zabura 86:11 (id: 10019086011)

## Zabura 86:12 (id: 10019086012)

## Zabura 86:13 (id: 10019086013)

## Zabura 86:14 (id: 10019086014)

## Zabura 86:15 (id: 10019086015)

## Zabura 86:16 (id: 10019086016)

## Zabura 106:1 (id: 10019106001)

1Yabi Ubangiji. Yi godiya ga Ubangiji, gama nagari ne shi; ƙaunarsa madawwamiya ce har abada.


## Zabura 106:2 (id: 10019106002)

2Wane ne zai furta manyan ayyukan Ubangiji ko yă furta cikakken yabonsa?


## Zabura 106:3 (id: 10019106003)

3Masu albarka ne waɗanda suke yin adalci, waɗanda kullum suke yin abin da yake daidai.


## Zabura 106:4 (id: 10019106004)

4Ka tuna da ni, ya Ubangiji, sa’ad da ka nuna alheri ga mutanenka, ka zo ka taimake ni sa’ad da ka cece su,


## Zabura 106:5 (id: 10019106005)

5don in ji daɗin nasarar zaɓaɓɓunka, don in sami rabo cikin farin cikin al’ummarka in kuma shiga gādonka cikin yin yabo.


## Zabura 106:6 (id: 10019106006)

6Mun yi zunubi, kamar yadda kakanninmu suka yi; mun yi ba daidai ba muka kuma aikata mugunta.


## Zabura 106:7 (id: 10019106007)

7Sa’ad da kakanninmu suke a Masar, ba su damu da mu’ujizanka ba; ba su tuna yawan alheranka ba, suka kuma yi tayarwa a teku, Jan Teku.


## Zabura 106:8 (id: 10019106008)

8Duk da haka ya cece su saboda sunansa, don yă sanar da ikonsa mai girma.


## Zabura 106:9 (id: 10019106009)

9Ya tsawata wa Jan Teku, ya kuwa bushe; ya bi da su ta cikin zurfafa sai ka ce a hamada.


## Zabura 106:10 (id: 10019106010)

10Ya cece su daga hannun maƙiyi; daga hannun abokin gāba ya fanshe su.


## Zabura 106:11 (id: 10019106011)

11Ruwaye suka rufe abokan gābansu; babu ko ɗaya da ya tsira.


## Zabura 106:12 (id: 10019106012)

12Sa’an nan suka gaskata alkawarinsa suka kuma rera yabonsa.


## Zabura 106:13 (id: 10019106013)

13Amma nan da nan suka manta abin da ya yi ba su kuwa jira shawararsa ba.


## Zabura 106:14 (id: 10019106014)

## Zabura 106:15 (id: 10019106015)

15Sai ya ba su abin da suka roƙa, amma ya aika musu da muguwar cuta.


## Zabura 106:16 (id: 10019106016)

16A sansani suka ji kishin Musa da kuma Haruna, wanda Ubangiji ya keɓe.


## Zabura 106:17 (id: 10019106017)

17Ƙasa ta buɗe ta haɗiye Datan ta binne iyalin Abiram.


## Zabura 106:18 (id: 10019106018)

18Wuta ta ƙuno a cikin mabiyansu; harshen wuta ya cinye mugaye.

## Zabura 106:20 (id: 10019106020)

## Zabura 106:25 (id: 10019106025)

25Suka yi gunaguni a tentunansu ba su kuwa yi biyayya da Ubangiji ba.


## Zabura 106:26 (id: 10019106026)

## Zabura 106:27 (id: 10019106027)

## Zabura 106:28 (id: 10019106028)

## Zabura 106:29 (id: 10019106029)

## Zabura 106:30 (id: 10019106030)

## Zabura 106:31 (id: 10019106031)

## Zabura 106:32 (id: 10019106032)

## Zabura 106:33 (id: 10019106033)

## Zabura 106:34 (id: 10019106034)

## Zabura 106:35 (id: 10019106035)

## Zabura 106:36 (id: 10019106036)

## Zabura 106:37 (id: 10019106037)

## Zabura 106:39 (id: 10019106039)

## Zabura 106:40 (id: 10019106040)

## Zabura 106:41 (id: 10019106041)

## Zabura 106:42 (id: 10019106042)

## Zabura 106:43 (id: 10019106043)

43Sau da yawa ya cece su, amma sun nace su yi tawaye suka kuma lalace cikin zunubinsu.

## Zabura 106:45 (id: 10019106045)

## Zabura 107:1 (id: 10019107001)

1Ku yi godiya ga Ubangiji, gama nagari ne shi; ƙaunarsa madawwamiya ce har abada.


## Zabura 107:2 (id: 10019107002)

## Zabura 107:3 (id: 10019107003)

## Zabura 107:4 (id: 10019107004)

## Zabura 107:5 (id: 10019107005)

## Zabura 107:6 (id: 10019107006)

## Zabura 107:7 (id: 10019107007)

## Zabura 107:8 (id: 10019107008)

## Zabura 107:9 (id: 10019107009)

9gama yana shayar da masu ƙishirwa yana kuma ƙosar da mayunwata da abubuwa masu kyau.


## Zabura 107:10 (id: 10019107010)

## Zabura 107:11 (id: 10019107011)

## Zabura 107:12 (id: 10019107012)

## Zabura 107:13 (id: 10019107013)

13Sa’an nan suka yi kuka ga Ubangiji cikin wahalarsu, ya kuwa cece su daga damuwarsu.


## Zabura 107:14 (id: 10019107014)

## Zabura 107:15 (id: 10019107015)

15Bari su yi godiya ga Ubangiji saboda ƙaunarsa marar ƙarewa da kuma ayyukansa masu banmamaki saboda mutane,


## Zabura 107:16 (id: 10019107016)

16gama ya farfashe ƙofofin tagulla ya kuma ragargaza ƙyamaren ƙarfe.


## Zabura 107:17 (id: 10019107017)

## Zabura 107:18 (id: 10019107018)

18Ba su so su ga abinci ba suka kuwa kai bakin mutuwa.


## Zabura 107:19 (id: 10019107019)

19Sa’an nan suka yi kuka ga Ubangiji cikin wahalarsu, ya kuwa cece su daga damuwarsu.


## Zabura 107:20 (id: 10019107020)

20Ya ba da umarninsa ya kuma warkar da su ya kuɓutar da su daga kabari.


## Zabura 107:21 (id: 10019107021)

21Bari su yi godiya ga Ubangiji saboda ƙaunarsa marar ƙarewa da kuma ayyukansa masu banmamaki saboda mutane.


## Zabura 107:22 (id: 10019107022)

22Bari su miƙa hadaya ta godiya su kuma ba da labarin ayyukansa da waƙoƙin farin ciki.


## Zabura 107:23 (id: 10019107023)

23Waɗansu suka yi tafiya a teku cikin jiragen ruwa; su ’yan kasuwa ne a manyan ruwaye.


## Zabura 107:24 (id: 10019107024)

## Zabura 107:25 (id: 10019107025)

25Gama ya yi magana ya kuma sa iska mai ƙarfi ta tashi ya ɗaga raƙuman ruwa sama.


## Zabura 107:26 (id: 10019107026)

26Suka hau zuwa sammai suka kuma tsinduma cikin zurfafa; a cikin azabarsu sai ƙarfin halinsu ya karai.


## Zabura 107:27 (id: 10019107027)

27Suka yi tangaɗi suna tuntuɓe kamar bugaggu; suka kuma kai ga ƙarshen dabararsu.


## Zabura 107:28 (id: 10019107028)

28Sai suka yi kuka ga Ubangiji cikin wahalarsu, ya kuwa fitar da su daga damuwarsu.


## Zabura 107:29 (id: 10019107029)

29Ya kwantar da hadiri suka yi tsit; raƙuman ruwan teku suka yi shiru.


## Zabura 107:30 (id: 10019107030)

30Suka yi murna sa’ad da wuri ya kwanta, ya kuma bishe su zuwa inda suka so su kai.


## Zabura 107:31 (id: 10019107031)

31Bari su yi godiya ga Ubangiji saboda ƙaunarsa marar ƙarewa da kuma ayyukansa masu banmamaki saboda mutane.


## Zabura 107:32 (id: 10019107032)

32Bari su ɗaukaka shi cikin taron mutane su kuma yabe shi cikin taron dattawa.


## Zabura 107:33 (id: 10019107033)

## Zabura 107:34 (id: 10019107034)

## Zabura 107:35 (id: 10019107035)

## Zabura 107:36 (id: 10019107036)

## Zabura 107:39 (id: 10019107039)

## Zabura 107:41 (id: 10019107041)

## Zabura 107:42 (id: 10019107042)

## Zabura 107:43 (id: 10019107043)

## Zabura 108:0 (id: 10019108000)

0Waƙa ce. Zabura ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 108:1 (id: 10019108001)

1Zuciyata tsayayyiya ce, ya Allah; zan rera in kuma yi kiɗi da dukan raina.


## Zabura 108:2 (id: 10019108002)

2Ku farka, garaya da molo! Zan farkar da rana.


## Zabura 108:3 (id: 10019108003)

3Zan yabe ka, ya Ubangiji, a cikin al’ummai; zan rera game da kai cikin mutane.


## Zabura 108:4 (id: 10019108004)

4Gama ƙaunarka da girma take, bisa fiye da sammai; amincinka ya kai sarari.


## Zabura 108:5 (id: 10019108005)

5A ɗaukaka ka, ya Allah, a bisa sammai, bari kuma ɗaukakarka ta kasance a bisa dukan duniya.


## Zabura 108:6 (id: 10019108006)

6Ka cece mu ka kuma taimake mu da hannunka na dama, domin waɗanda kake ƙauna su kuɓuta.


## Zabura 108:7 (id: 10019108007)

7Allah ya yi magana daga wurinsa mai tsarki, “Ciki nasara zan raba Shekem in kuma auna Kwarin Sukkot.


## Zabura 108:8 (id: 10019108008)

8Gileyad nawa ne, Manasse nawa ne; Efraim shi hulan kwanona, Yahuda ne sandana na sarauta.


## Zabura 108:9 (id: 10019108009)

9Mowab shi ne kwanon wankina, a kan Edom zan jefa takalmina; a bisa Filistiya zan yi kirari cikin nasara.”


## Zabura 108:10 (id: 10019108010)

10Wa zai kawo ni birni mai katanga? Wa zai jagorance ni zuwa Edom?


## Zabura 108:11 (id: 10019108011)

11Ba kai ba ne, ya Allah, kai da ka ƙi mu ba ka kuwa fita tare da mayaƙanmu?


## Zabura 108:12 (id: 10019108012)

12Ka ba mu taimako a kan abokin gāba, gama taimakon mutum banza ne.


## Zabura 108:13 (id: 10019108013)

13Tare da Allah za mu yi nasara, zai kuma tattaka abokan gābanmu.


## Zabura 109:0 (id: 10019109000)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Ta Dawuda. Zabura ce.


## Zabura 109:1 (id: 10019109001)

1Ya Allah, wanda nake yabo, kada ka yi shiru,


## Zabura 109:2 (id: 10019109002)

2gama mugaye da masu ruɗu sun buɗe bakunansu a kaina; sun yi magana a kaina da harsunan ƙarya.


## Zabura 109:3 (id: 10019109003)

3Da kalmomin ƙiyayya sun kewaye ni; sun tasar mini ba dalili.


## Zabura 109:4 (id: 10019109004)

4A maimakon ƙauna sun sāka mini da zargi, amma ni mutum ne mai addu’a.


## Zabura 109:5 (id: 10019109005)

5Sun sāka mini alheri da mugunta, ƙauna kuma da ƙiyayya.


## Zabura 109:6 (id: 10019109006)

6Ka naɗa mugun mutum yă yi hamayya da shi; bari mai zargi yă tsaya a hannun damansa.


## Zabura 109:7 (id: 10019109007)

7Sa’ad da aka yi masa shari’a, bari a same shi da laifi, bari kuma addu’o’insa su hukunta shi.


## Zabura 109:8 (id: 10019109008)

8Bari kwanakinsa su zama kaɗan; bari wani yă ɗauki wurinsa na shugabanci.


## Zabura 109:9 (id: 10019109009)

9Bari ’ya’yansa su zama marayu matarsa kuma gwauruwa.


## Zabura 109:10 (id: 10019109010)

10Bari ’ya’yansa su zama masu yawo suna bara; bari a yi ta koransu daga gidajensu da suke kufai.


## Zabura 109:11 (id: 10019109011)

11Bari mai binsa bashi yă ƙwace dukan abin da yake da shi; bari baƙi su washe amfanin aikinsa.


## Zabura 109:12 (id: 10019109012)

12Bari kada kowa yă yi masa alheri ko yă ji tausayin marayunsa.


## Zabura 109:13 (id: 10019109013)

13Bari duk zuriyarsa su mutu, a shafe sunayensu daga tsara mai zuwa.


## Zabura 109:14 (id: 10019109014)

14Bari a tuna da laifin kakanninsa a gaban Ubangiji; bari kada a taɓa shafe zunubin mahaifiyarsa.


## Zabura 109:15 (id: 10019109015)

15Bari zunubansu su kasance a gaban Ubangiji kullum, don yă sa a manta da su daga duniya.


## Zabura 109:16 (id: 10019109016)

16Gama bai taɓa yin tunanin yin alheri ba, amma ya tsananta wa matalauta da mabukata da kuma masu fid da zuciya har suka mutu.


## Zabura 109:17 (id: 10019109017)

17Yana jin daɗin la’antarwa, bari yă dawo a kansa; ba ya son sa albarka, bari kada kowa yă sa masa albarka.


## Zabura 109:18 (id: 10019109018)

18Ya sa la’antarwa kamar rigarsa; ta shiga cikin jikinsa kamar ruwa, cikin ƙasusuwansa kamar mai.


## Zabura 109:19 (id: 10019109019)

19Bari tă zama kamar mayafin da aka ɗaura kewaye da shi, kamar ɗamara da aka ɗaura kewaye da shi har abada.


## Zabura 109:20 (id: 10019109020)

20Bari wannan yă zama sakayyar Ubangiji ga masu zargina, ga waɗanda suke mugayen maganganu a kaina.


## Zabura 109:21 (id: 10019109021)

21Amma kai, ya Ubangiji Mai Iko Duka, ka yi da ni da kyau saboda sunanka; ta alherin ƙaunarka, ka cece ni.


## Zabura 109:22 (id: 10019109022)

22Gama ni matalauci ne mai bukata kuma, zuciyata kuwa ta yi rauni a cikina.


## Zabura 109:23 (id: 10019109023)

23Na ɓace kamar inuwar yamma; ana kakkaɓe ni kamar fāra.


## Zabura 109:24 (id: 10019109024)

24Gwiwoyina suna mutuwa saboda azumi; jikina ya rame ba kuma ƙarfi.


## Zabura 109:25 (id: 10019109025)

25Na zama abin dariya ga masu zargina; sa’ad da suka gan ni, suka kaɗa kawunansu.


## Zabura 109:26 (id: 10019109026)

26Ka taimake ni, ya Ubangiji Allahna; ka cece ni bisa ga ƙaunarka.


## Zabura 109:27 (id: 10019109027)

27Bari su san cewa hannunka ne, cewa kai ne, ya Ubangiji, ka yi shi.


## Zabura 109:28 (id: 10019109028)

28Za su iya la’anta, amma kai za ka sa albarka; sa’ad da suka tasar za su sha kunya, amma bawanka zai yi farin ciki.


## Zabura 109:29 (id: 10019109029)

29Masu zargina za su sha kunya za a rufe su da kunya kamar mayafi.


## Zabura 109:30 (id: 10019109030)

30Da bakina zan ɗaukaka Ubangiji sosai; cikin babban taro zan yabe shi.


## Zabura 109:31 (id: 10019109031)

31Gama yana tsaya a hannun damar mai bukata, don yă cece ransa daga waɗanda suke hukunta shi.


## Zabura 110:0 (id: 10019110000)

0Ta Dawuda. Zabura ce.


## Zabura 110:1 (id: 10019110001)

1Ubangiji ya ce wa Ubangijina, “Zauna a hannun damana, sai na sa abokan gābanka su zama matashin sawunka.”


## Zabura 110:2 (id: 10019110002)

2Ubangiji zai fadada ikon sandan mulkinka daga Sihiyona za ka yi mulki a tsakiyar abokan gābanka.


## Zabura 110:3 (id: 10019110003)

3Rundunarka za su so yin yaƙi a ranarka ta yaƙi. Saye cikin ɗaukaka mai tsarki, daga cikin ciki na safiya za ka karɓi raɓar ƙuruciyarka.


## Zabura 110:4 (id: 10019110004)

4Ubangiji ya rantse ba zai kuma canja zuciyarsa ba, “Kai firist ne har abada, bisa ga tsarin Melkizedek.”


## Zabura 110:5 (id: 10019110005)

5Ubangiji yana a hannun damanka; zai murƙushe sarakuna a ranar fushinsa.


## Zabura 110:6 (id: 10019110006)

6Zai hukunta al’ummai, yana tarawa gawawwaki yana kuma murƙushe masu mulkin dukan duniya.


## Zabura 110:7 (id: 10019110007)

7Zai sha daga rafin da yake kusa da hanya; saboda haka zai ɗaga kansa sama.


## Zabura 111:1 (id: 10019111001)

1Yabi Ubangiji. Zan ɗaukaka Ubangiji da dukan zuciyata a cikin majalisar masu aikata gaskiya da kuma a cikin taro.


## Zabura 111:2 (id: 10019111002)

2Ayyukan Ubangiji da girma suke; dukan waɗanda suke farin ciki da su suna tunaninsu.


## Zabura 111:3 (id: 10019111003)

3Ayyukansa masu ɗaukaka da kuma daraja ne, adalcinsa kuma zai dawwama har abada.


## Zabura 111:4 (id: 10019111004)

4Ya sa a tuna da abubuwa masu banmamaki nasa; Ubangiji mai alheri ne mai tausayi kuma.


## Zabura 111:5 (id: 10019111005)

5Yana ba da abinci ga waɗanda suke tsoronsa; yakan tuna da alkawarinsa har abada.


## Zabura 111:6 (id: 10019111006)

6Ya nuna wa mutanensa ikon ayyukansa, yana ba su ƙasashen waɗansu al’ummai.


## Zabura 111:7 (id: 10019111007)

7Ayyukan hannuwansa masu aminci ne da kuma gaskiya; dukan farillansa abin dogara ne.


## Zabura 111:8 (id: 10019111008)

8Tsayayyu ne har abada abadin, ana yinsu cikin aminci da kuma gaskiya.


## Zabura 111:9 (id: 10019111009)

9Yana ba da ceto ga mutanensa; ya kafa alkawarinsa har abada, tsarki da banrazana ne sunansa.


## Zabura 111:10 (id: 10019111010)

10Tsoron Ubangiji ne mafarin hikima; dukan waɗanda suke bin farillansa suna da ganewa mai kyau. Gare shi ne madawwamin yabo.


## Zabura 112:1 (id: 10019112001)

1Yabi Ubangiji. Mai albarka ne mutumin da yake tsoron Ubangiji, wanda yakan sami farin ciki mai girma a umarnansa.


## Zabura 112:2 (id: 10019112002)

2’Ya’yansa za su zama manya a ƙasar; tsaran masu aikata gaskiya za su sami albarka.


## Zabura 112:3 (id: 10019112003)

3Wadata da arziki suna a cikin gidansa, adalcinsa zai dawwama har abada.


## Zabura 112:4 (id: 10019112004)

4Ko a cikin duhu haske kan haskaka wa mai aikata gaskiya, mai alheri mai tausayi da kuma mai adalci.


## Zabura 112:5 (id: 10019112005)

5Abu mai kyau zai zo masa shi da yake kyauta yake kuma ba da bashi hannu sake, shi da yake yin al’amuransa cikin gaskiya.


## Zabura 112:6 (id: 10019112006)

6Tabbatacce ba zai taɓa jijjigu ba; za a tuna da mai adalci har abada.


## Zabura 112:7 (id: 10019112007)

7Ba zai ji tsoron labari marar daɗi ba; zuciyarsa tsayayyiya ce, tana dogara ga Ubangiji.


## Zabura 112:8 (id: 10019112008)

8Zuciyarsa tana da kāriya, ba zai ji tsoro ba; a ƙarshe zai zama mai nasara a kan maƙiyansa.


## Zabura 112:9 (id: 10019112009)

9Ya rarraba kyautansa ga matalauta, adalcinsa zai dawwama har abada; za a ɗaga ƙahonsa sama da bangirma.


## Zabura 112:10 (id: 10019112010)

10Mugun mutum zai gani yă kuma yi fushi, zai ciza haƙora yă kuma lalace; sha’awace\-sha’awacen mugaye za su zo ga ƙarshe.


## Zabura 113:1 (id: 10019113001)

1Yabi Ubangiji. Ku yabe shi, ya ku bayin Ubangiji, ku yabi sunan Ubangiji.


## Zabura 113:2 (id: 10019113002)

2Bari a yabi sunan Ubangiji, yanzu da har abada kuma.


## Zabura 113:3 (id: 10019113003)

3Daga fitowar rana zuwa inda take fāɗuwa, a yabi sunan Ubangiji.


## Zabura 113:4 (id: 10019113004)

4Ana ɗaukaka Ubangiji a bisa dukan al’ummai, ɗaukakarsa a bisa sammai


## Zabura 113:5 (id: 10019113005)

5Wane ne yake kamar Ubangiji Allahnmu, Wannan mai zama a kursiyi can bisa,


## Zabura 113:6 (id: 10019113006)

6wanda yake sunkuya yă dubi sammai da duniya?


## Zabura 113:7 (id: 10019113007)

7Yakan tā da matalauta daga ƙura yakan ɗaga mabukata daga tarin toka;


## Zabura 113:8 (id: 10019113008)

8ya zaunar da su tare da sarakuna, tare da sarakunan mutanensu.


## Zabura 113:9 (id: 10019113009)

9Yakan zaunar da matar da ba haihuwa a gidanta ta zama kamar mahaifiyar ’ya’ya mai farin ciki. Yabi Ubangiji.


## Zabura 114:1 (id: 10019114001)

1Sa’ad da Isra’ila ya fito daga Masar, gidan Yaƙub daga mutane masu baƙon harshe,


## Zabura 114:2 (id: 10019114002)

2Yahuda ya zama wuri mai tsarki na Allah, Isra’ila mallakarsa.


## Zabura 114:3 (id: 10019114003)

3Teku ya kalla ya kuma gudu, Urdun ya juye da baya;


## Zabura 114:4 (id: 10019114004)

4duwatsu suka yi tsalle kamar raguna, tuddai kamar tumaki.


## Zabura 114:5 (id: 10019114005)

5Me ya sa, ya teku, kika gudu, Ya Urdun, ka juya baya,


## Zabura 114:6 (id: 10019114006)

6ku duwatsu, kuka yi tsalle kamar raguna, ku tuddai, kamar tumaki?


## Zabura 114:7 (id: 10019114007)

7Ki yi rawar jiki, ya duniya, a gaban Ubangiji, a gaban Allah na Yaƙub,


## Zabura 114:8 (id: 10019114008)

8wanda ya juye dutse ya zama tafki, dutse mai ƙarfi zuwa maɓulɓulan ruwa.


## Zabura 115:1 (id: 10019115001)

1Ba gare mu ba, ya Ubangiji, ba gare mu ba amma ga sunanka yă sami ɗaukaka, saboda ƙaunarka da amincinka.


## Zabura 115:2 (id: 10019115002)

2Me ya sa al’ummai suke cewa, “Ina Allahnsu?”


## Zabura 115:3 (id: 10019115003)

3Allahnmu yana a sama; yana yin duk abin da ya ga dama.


## Zabura 115:4 (id: 10019115004)

4Amma gumakansu azurfa da zinariya ne, da hannuwan mutane suka yi.


## Zabura 115:5 (id: 10019115005)

5Suna da bakuna, amma ba sa magana, idanu, amma ba sa gani;


## Zabura 115:6 (id: 10019115006)

6suna da kunnuwa, amma ba sa ji, hanci, amma ba sa jin ƙanshi


## Zabura 115:7 (id: 10019115007)

7suna da hannuwa, amma ba sa iya taɓa kome, ƙafafu, amma ba sa iya tafiya; ba sa iya ce wani abu da maƙogwaronsu.


## Zabura 115:8 (id: 10019115008)

8Masu yinsu za su zama kamar su, haka kuma zai kasance da duk wanda ya dogara da su.


## Zabura 115:9 (id: 10019115009)

9Ya gidan Isra’ila, ku dogara ga Ubangiji, shi ne mai taimakonsu da garkuwarsu.


## Zabura 115:10 (id: 10019115010)

10Ya gidan Haruna, ku dogara ga Ubangiji, shi ne mai taimakonsu da garkuwarsu.


## Zabura 115:11 (id: 10019115011)

11Ku da kuke tsoronsa, ku dogara ga Ubangiji, shi ne mai taimakonsu da garkuwarsu.


## Zabura 115:12 (id: 10019115012)

12Ubangiji yakan tuna da mu zai kuma albarkace mu. Zai albarkace gidan Isra’ila, zai albarkace gidan Haruna,


## Zabura 115:13 (id: 10019115013)

13zai albarkace waɗanda suke tsoron Ubangiji, manya da ƙanana duka.


## Zabura 115:14 (id: 10019115014)

14Bari Ubangiji yă sa ku ƙaru, da ku da ’ya’yanku.


## Zabura 115:15 (id: 10019115015)

15Bari Ubangiji yă albarkace ku, shi Mahalicci sama da ƙasa.


## Zabura 115:16 (id: 10019115016)

16Saman sammai na Ubangiji ne, amma ya ba da duniya ga mutum.


## Zabura 115:17 (id: 10019115017)

17Ba matattu ba ne masu yabon Ubangiji, waɗanda suke gangara zuwa wurin da ake shiru;


## Zabura 115:18 (id: 10019115018)

18mu ne masu ɗaukaka Ubangiji, yanzu da har abada kuma. Yabi Ubangiji.


## Zabura 116:1 (id: 10019116001)

1Ina ƙaunar Ubangiji, gama ya ji muryata; ya ji kukata ta neman jinƙai.


## Zabura 116:2 (id: 10019116002)

2Domin ya juye kunnensa gare ni, zan kira gare shi muddin ina da rai.


## Zabura 116:3 (id: 10019116003)

3Igiyoyin mutuwa sun shaƙe ni, wahalar kabari sun zo a kaina; na cika da wahala da ɓacin rai.


## Zabura 116:4 (id: 10019116004)

4Sai na kira ga sunan Ubangiji na ce, “Ya Ubangiji, ka cece ni!”


## Zabura 116:5 (id: 10019116005)

5Ubangiji mai alheri ne da kuma mai adalci; Allahnmu yana cike da tausayi.


## Zabura 116:6 (id: 10019116006)

6Ubangiji yana tsare masu tawali’u; sa’ad da nake cikin tsananin bukata, ya cece ni.


## Zabura 116:7 (id: 10019116007)

7Ka kwantar da hankali, ya raina, gama Ubangiji mai alheri ne a gare ka.


## Zabura 116:8 (id: 10019116008)

8Gama kai, ya Ubangiji, ka ceci raina daga mutuwa, idanuna daga hawaye, ƙafafuna daga tuntuɓe,


## Zabura 116:9 (id: 10019116009)

9don in iya tafiya a gaban Ubangiji a ƙasar masu rai.


## Zabura 116:10 (id: 10019116010)

10Na gaskata, saboda haka na ce, “An azabtar da ni ƙwarai.”


## Zabura 116:11 (id: 10019116011)

11Kuma cikin rikicewana na ce, “Dukan mutane maƙaryata ne.”


## Zabura 116:12 (id: 10019116012)

12Yaya zan sāka wa Ubangiji saboda dukan alherinsa gare ni?


## Zabura 116:13 (id: 10019116013)

13Zan daga kwaf na ceto in kuma kira ga sunan Ubangiji.


## Zabura 116:14 (id: 10019116014)

14Zan cika alkawurana ga Ubangiji a gaban dukan mutanensa.


## Zabura 116:15 (id: 10019116015)

15Abu mai daraja a gaban Ubangiji shi ne mutuwar tsarkakansa.


## Zabura 116:16 (id: 10019116016)

16Ya Ubangiji, da gaske ni bawanka ne; ni bawanka ne, ɗan baiwarka; ka ’yantar da ni daga sarƙoƙi.


## Zabura 116:17 (id: 10019116017)

17Zan yi hadayar godiya gare ka in kuma kira bisa sunan Ubangiji.


## Zabura 116:18 (id: 10019116018)

18Zan cika alkawurana ga Ubangiji a gaban dukan mutanensa,


## Zabura 116:19 (id: 10019116019)

19a filayen gidan Ubangiji, a tsakiyarki, ya Urushalima. Yabi Ubangiji.


## Zabura 117:1 (id: 10019117001)

1Yabi Ubangiji, dukanku al’umma; ku ɗaukaka shi, dukanku mutane.


## Zabura 117:2 (id: 10019117002)

2Gama ƙaunarsa da girma take gare mu, amincin Ubangiji kuma madawwami ne har abada. Yabi Ubangiji.


## Zabura 118:1 (id: 10019118001)

1Ku gode wa Ubangiji, gama nagari ne shi; ƙaunarsa madawwamiya ce har abada.


## Zabura 118:2 (id: 10019118002)

2Bari Isra’ila yă ce, “Ƙaunarsa madawwamiya ce har abada.”


## Zabura 118:3 (id: 10019118003)

3Bari gidan Haruna yă ce, “Ƙaunarsa madawwamiya ce har abada.”


## Zabura 118:4 (id: 10019118004)

4Bari waɗanda suke tsoron Ubangiji su ce, “Ƙaunarsa madawwamiya ce har abada.”


## Zabura 118:5 (id: 10019118005)

5Cikin azabata na yi kuka ga Ubangiji, ya kuwa amsa ta wurin ’yantar da ni.


## Zabura 118:6 (id: 10019118006)

6Ubangiji yana tare da ni; ba zan ji tsoro ba. Me mutum zai yi mini?


## Zabura 118:7 (id: 10019118007)

7Ubangiji yana tare da ni; shi ne mai taimakona. Zan yi nasara a kan abokan gābana.


## Zabura 118:8 (id: 10019118008)

8Ya fi kyau in nemi mafaka a wurin Ubangiji da in dogara ga mutum.


## Zabura 118:9 (id: 10019118009)

9Ya fi kyau in nemi mafaka a wurin Ubangiji da in dogara ga sarakuna.


## Zabura 118:10 (id: 10019118010)

10Dukan al’ummai sun kewaye ni, amma a cikin sunan Ubangiji na hallaka su.


## Zabura 118:11 (id: 10019118011)

11Sun kewaye ni a kowane gefe, amma a cikin sunan Ubangiji na hallaka su.


## Zabura 118:12 (id: 10019118012)

12Sun rufe ni kamar ƙudan zuma, amma suka mutu nan da nan kamar ƙayayyuwa masu cin wuta; a cikin sunan Ubangiji na hallaka su.


## Zabura 118:13 (id: 10019118013)

13An ture ni baya na kusa fāɗuwa, amma Ubangiji ya taimake ni.


## Zabura 118:14 (id: 10019118014)

14Ubangiji ne ƙarfina da waƙata; ya zama mai cetona.


## Zabura 118:15 (id: 10019118015)

15Sowa ta farin ciki da nasara sun fito a cikin tentunan adalai, “Hannun dama na Ubangiji ya aikata manyan abubuwa!


## Zabura 118:16 (id: 10019118016)

16An ɗaga hannun dama na Ubangiji sama; hannun dama na Ubangiji ya aikata manyan abubuwa!”


## Zabura 118:17 (id: 10019118017)

17Ba zan mutu ba amma zan rayu, zan kuwa yi shelar abin da Ubangiji ya yi.


## Zabura 118:18 (id: 10019118018)

18Ubangiji ya hore ni sosai, amma bai ba da ni ga mutuwa ba.


## Zabura 118:19 (id: 10019118019)

19Buɗe mini ƙofofin adalci; zan shiga in kuma yi godiya ga Ubangiji.


## Zabura 118:20 (id: 10019118020)

20Wannan ne ƙofar Ubangiji inda adalai za su shiga.


## Zabura 118:21 (id: 10019118021)

21Zan yi maka godiya, domin ka amsa mini; ka zama mai cetona.


## Zabura 118:22 (id: 10019118022)

22Zabura Dutsen da magina suka ƙi ya zama mai amfani;


## Zabura 118:23 (id: 10019118023)

23Ubangiji ne ya yi haka, kuma abu mai daraja ne a idanunmu.


## Zabura 118:24 (id: 10019118024)

24Wannan ita ce ranar da Ubangiji ya yi; bari mu yi farin ciki mu kuma yi murna a cikinta.


## Zabura 118:25 (id: 10019118025)

25Ya Ubangiji, ka cece mu; Ya Ubangiji, ka ba mu nasara.


## Zabura 118:26 (id: 10019118026)

26Mai albarka ne shi mai zuwa cikin sunan Ubangiji. Daga gidan Ubangiji muna sa muku albarka.


## Zabura 118:27 (id: 10019118027)

27Ubangiji shi ne Allah ya kuma sa haskensa ya haskaka a kanmu. Da kwanoni a hannunmu, muka shiga jerin gwanon biki ku kai su ƙahoni na bagade.


## Zabura 118:28 (id: 10019118028)

28Kai ne Allahna, zan kuwa yi maka godiya; kai ne Allahna, zan kuwa ɗaukaka ka.


## Zabura 118:29 (id: 10019118029)

29Ku gode wa Ubangiji, gama nagari ne shi; ƙaunarsa madawwamiya ce har abada.


## Zabura 119:1 (id: 10019119001)

1Albarka ta tabbata ga waɗanda rayuwarsu ba ta da abin zargi, waɗanda suke tafiya bisa ga dokar Ubangiji.


## Zabura 119:2 (id: 10019119002)

2Albarka ta tabbata ga waɗanda suke kiyaye ƙa’idodinsa suke kuma nemansa da dukan zuciyarsu.


## Zabura 119:3 (id: 10019119003)

3Ba sa yin wani abin da ba daidai ba; suna tafiya a hanyoyinsa.


## Zabura 119:4 (id: 10019119004)

4Ka shimfiɗa farillan da dole a yi biyayya da su.


## Zabura 119:5 (id: 10019119005)

5Kash, da a ce hanyoyina tsayayyu ne a yin biyayya da ƙa’idodinka mana!


## Zabura 119:6 (id: 10019119006)

6Da ba zan sha kunya ba sa’ad da na lura da dukan umarnanka.


## Zabura 119:7 (id: 10019119007)

7Zan yabe ka da zuciya ta gaskiya yayinda nake koyon dokokinka masu adalci.


## Zabura 119:8 (id: 10019119008)

8Zan yi biyayya da ƙa’idodinka; kada ka yashe ni ɗungum.


## Zabura 119:9 (id: 10019119009)

9Yaya matashi zai kiyaye hanyarsa da tsabta? Sai ta yin rayuwa bisa ga maganarka.


## Zabura 119:10 (id: 10019119010)

10Na neme ka da dukan zuciyata; kada ka bar ni in kauce daga umarnanka.


## Zabura 119:11 (id: 10019119011)

11Na ɓoye maganarka a cikin zuciyata don kada in yi maka zunubi.


## Zabura 119:12 (id: 10019119012)

12Yabo ya tabbata gare ka, ya Ubangiji; ka koya mini ƙa’idodinka.


## Zabura 119:13 (id: 10019119013)

13Da leɓunana na ba da labarin dukan dokokin da suka fito bakinka.


## Zabura 119:14 (id: 10019119014)

14Na yi farin ciki da bin farillanka yadda mutum yakan yi farin ciki da arziki mai yawa.


## Zabura 119:15 (id: 10019119015)

15Na yi tunani a kan farillanka na kuma lura da hanyoyinka.


## Zabura 119:16 (id: 10019119016)

16Na yi murna a cikin ƙa’idodinka; ba zan ƙyale maganarka ba.


## Zabura 119:17 (id: 10019119017)

17Ka yi alheri ga bawanka, zan kuwa rayu; zan yi biyayya da maganarka.


## Zabura 119:18 (id: 10019119018)

18Ka buɗe idanuna don in iya gani abubuwan banmamaki a cikin dokarka.


## Zabura 119:19 (id: 10019119019)

19Ni baƙo ne a duniya; kada ka ɓoye mini umarnanka.


## Zabura 119:20 (id: 10019119020)

20Zuciyata ta ƙosa saboda marmari don dokokinka koyaushe.


## Zabura 119:21 (id: 10019119021)

21Ka tsawata wa masu fariya, waɗanda suke la’anta waɗanda kuma suka kauce daga umarnanka.


## Zabura 119:22 (id: 10019119022)

22Ka cire mini ba’a da reni, gama ina kiyaye farillanka.


## Zabura 119:23 (id: 10019119023)

23Ko da yake masu mulki sun zauna tare suna ɓata mini suna, bawanka zai yi tunani a kan ƙa’idodinka.


## Zabura 119:24 (id: 10019119024)

24Farillanka ne abin farin cikina; su ne mashawartana.


## Zabura 119:25 (id: 10019119025)

25An kwantar da ni ƙasa cikin ƙura; ka kiyaye raina bisa ga maganarka.


## Zabura 119:26 (id: 10019119026)

26Na ba da labari hanyoyina ka kuma amsa mini; ka koya mini ƙa’idodinka.


## Zabura 119:27 (id: 10019119027)

27Bari in gane koyarwar farillanka; sa’an nan zan yi tunani a kan abubuwan banmamakinka.


## Zabura 119:28 (id: 10019119028)

28Raina ya gaji da baƙin ciki; ka ƙarfafa ni bisa ga maganarka.


## Zabura 119:29 (id: 10019119029)

29Ka kiyaye ni daga hanyoyin ruɗu; ka yi mini alheri ta wurin dokokinka.


## Zabura 119:30 (id: 10019119030)

30Na zaɓi hanyar gaskiya; na sa zuciyata a kan dokokinka.


## Zabura 119:31 (id: 10019119031)

31Na riƙe farillanka kankan, ya Ubangiji; kada ka sa in sha kunya.


## Zabura 119:32 (id: 10019119032)

32Ina gudu a kan hanyar umarnanka, gama ka ’yantar da zuciyata.


## Zabura 119:33 (id: 10019119033)

33Ka koya mini Ya Ubangiji, don in bi ƙa’idodinka; sa’an nan zan kiyaye su har ƙarshe.


## Zabura 119:34 (id: 10019119034)

34Ka ba ni ganewa, zan kuwa kiyaye dokarka in kuma yi biyayya da ita da dukan zuciyata.


## Zabura 119:35 (id: 10019119035)

35Ka bi da ni a hanyar umarnanka, gama a can zan sami farin ciki.


## Zabura 119:36 (id: 10019119036)

36Ka juye zuciyata wajen farillanka ba wajen riba ta sonkai ba.


## Zabura 119:37 (id: 10019119037)

37Ka juye idanuna daga abubuwa marasa amfani; ka kiyaye raina bisa ga maganarka.


## Zabura 119:38 (id: 10019119038)

38Ka cika alkawarinka ga bawanka, saboda a ji tsoronka.


## Zabura 119:39 (id: 10019119039)

39Ka kawar da shan kunyar da nake tsoro, gama dokokinka nagari ne.


## Zabura 119:40 (id: 10019119040)

40Ina marmarin farillanka ƙwarai! Ka kiyaye raina cikin adalcinka.


## Zabura 119:41 (id: 10019119041)

41Bari ƙaunarka marar ƙarewa ta zo gare ni, ya Ubangiji, cetonka bisa ga alkawarinka;


## Zabura 119:42 (id: 10019119042)

42sa’an nan zan amsa wa masu cin mutuncina, gama na dogara ga maganarka.


## Zabura 119:43 (id: 10019119043)

43Kada ka ƙwace maganarka daga bakina, gama na sa zuciyata a dokokinka.


## Zabura 119:44 (id: 10019119044)

44Kullayaumi zan yi biyayya da dokokinka, har abada abadin.


## Zabura 119:45 (id: 10019119045)

45Zan yi ta yawo a sake gama na nemi farillanka.


## Zabura 119:46 (id: 10019119046)

46Zan yi maganar farillanka a gaban sarakuna ba kuwa za a kunyata ni ba,


## Zabura 119:47 (id: 10019119047)

47gama ina farin ciki da umarnanka saboda ina ƙaunarsu.


## Zabura 119:48 (id: 10019119048)

48Na ɗaga hannuwana ga umarnanka, waɗanda nake ƙauna, ina kuma tunani a kan ƙa’idodinka.


## Zabura 119:49 (id: 10019119049)

49Tuna da maganarka ga bawanka, gama ka ba ni bege.


## Zabura 119:50 (id: 10019119050)

50Ta’aziyyata cikin wahalata ita ce alkawarinka yana kiyaye raina.


## Zabura 119:51 (id: 10019119051)

51Masu fariya suna yi mini ba’a ba tare da an hana su ba, amma ban rabu da dokar ba.


## Zabura 119:52 (id: 10019119052)

52Na tuna da dokokinka na tun dā, ya Ubangiji, na kuwa sami ta’aziyya a cikinsu.


## Zabura 119:53 (id: 10019119053)

53Fushi ya kama ni saboda mugaye, waɗanda suka keta dokokinka.


## Zabura 119:54 (id: 10019119054)

54Ƙa’idodinka su ne kan waƙata a duk inda na sauka.


## Zabura 119:55 (id: 10019119055)

55Da dare na tuna da sunanka, ya Ubangiji, zan kuwa kiyaye dokarka.


## Zabura 119:56 (id: 10019119056)

56Wannan shi ne na saba yi, ina yin biyayya da farillanka.


## Zabura 119:57 (id: 10019119057)

57Kai ne rabona, ya Ubangiji; na yi alkawarin in kiyaye maganarka.


## Zabura 119:58 (id: 10019119058)

58Na nemi fuskarka da dukan zuciyata; ka yi mini alheri bisa ga alkawarinka.


## Zabura 119:59 (id: 10019119059)

59Na lura da hanyoyina na kuma mayar da matakaina ga farillanka.


## Zabura 119:60 (id: 10019119060)

60Zan gaggauta ba zan ɓata lokaci ba in yi biyayya da umarnanka.


## Zabura 119:61 (id: 10019119061)

61Ko da yake mugaye sun ɗaura ni da igiyoyi, ba zan manta da dokokinka ba.


## Zabura 119:62 (id: 10019119062)

62Da tsakar dare nakan tashi in gode maka saboda dokokinka masu adalci.


## Zabura 119:63 (id: 10019119063)

63Ni aboki ne ga duk mai tsoronka, ga duk wanda yake bin farillanka.


## Zabura 119:64 (id: 10019119064)

64Duniya ta cika da ƙaunarka, ya Ubangiji; ka koya mini ƙa’idodinka.


## Zabura 119:65 (id: 10019119065)

65Ka yi wa bawanka alheri bisa ga maganarka, ya Ubangiji.


## Zabura 119:66 (id: 10019119066)

66Ka koya mini sani da kuma hukunci mai kyau, gama na gaskata a umarnanka.


## Zabura 119:67 (id: 10019119067)

67Kafin in sha wahala na kauce, amma yanzu ina biyayya da maganarka.


## Zabura 119:68 (id: 10019119068)

68Kai nagari ne, kuma abin da kake yi yana da kyau; ka koya mini ƙa’idodinka.


## Zabura 119:69 (id: 10019119069)

69Ko da yake masu fariya sun shafe ni da ƙarairayi, na kiyaye farillanka da dukan zuciyata.


## Zabura 119:70 (id: 10019119070)

70Zukatansu sun yi tauri da kuma marasa tausayi amma ina farin ciki a dokarka.


## Zabura 119:71 (id: 10019119071)

71Ya yi kyau da na sha wahala don in koyi ƙa’idodinka.


## Zabura 119:72 (id: 10019119072)

72Doka daga bakinka ya fi mini daraja fiye da azurfa da zinariya guda dubu.


## Zabura 119:73 (id: 10019119073)

73Hannuwanka ne suka yi suka kuma siffanta ni; ka ba ni ganewa don in koyi umarnanka.


## Zabura 119:74 (id: 10019119074)

74Bari waɗanda suke tsoronka su yi farin ciki sa’ad da suke gan ni, gama na sa zuciyata a maganarka.

## Zabura 120:0 (id: 10019120000)

0Waƙar haurawa.


## Zabura 120:1 (id: 10019120001)

1Na yi kira ga Ubangiji cikin damuwata, ya kuwa amsa mini.


## Zabura 120:2 (id: 10019120002)

2Ka cece ni, ya Ubangiji, daga leɓuna masu ƙarya da kuma daga harsuna masu yaudara.


## Zabura 120:3 (id: 10019120003)

## Zabura 120:4 (id: 10019120004)

## Zabura 120:5 (id: 10019120005)

5Kaitona da nake zama a Meshek, da nake zama a ciki tentunan Kedar!


## Zabura 120:6 (id: 10019120006)

6Da daɗewa na zauna a cikin waɗanda suke ƙin salama.


## Zabura 120:7 (id: 10019120007)

## Zabura 121:0 (id: 10019121000)

0Waƙar haurawa.


## Zabura 121:1 (id: 10019121001)

1Na tā da idanuna zuwa wajen tuddai, ta ina ne taimakona zai zo?


## Zabura 121:2 (id: 10019121002)

2Taimakona zai zo daga wurin Ubangiji, wanda ya kafa sama da ƙasa.


## Zabura 121:3 (id: 10019121003)

## Zabura 121:4 (id: 10019121004)

## Zabura 121:5 (id: 10019121005)

5Ubangiji yana tsaronka, Ubangiji ne inuwa a hannun damanka;


## Zabura 121:6 (id: 10019121006)

6rana ba za tă buge ka cikin yini ba balle wata da dare.


## Zabura 121:7 (id: 10019121007)

7Ubangiji zai kiyaye ka daga dukan masifa, zai tsare ranka;


## Zabura 121:8 (id: 10019121008)

8Ubangiji zai kiyaye shigarka da fitarka yanzu da har abada kuma.


## Zabura 122:0 (id: 10019122000)

0Waƙar haurawa. Ta Dawuda.


## Zabura 122:1 (id: 10019122001)

1Na yi farin ciki tare da waɗanda suka ce da ni, “Bari mu tafi gidan Ubangiji.”


## Zabura 122:2 (id: 10019122002)

2Ƙafafunmu suna tsaye a ƙofofinki, ya Urushalima.


## Zabura 122:3 (id: 10019122003)

3An gina Urushalima kamar birnin da aka yi a harhaɗe wuri guda.


## Zabura 122:4 (id: 10019122004)

4A can ne kabilu suke haurawa, kabilan Ubangiji, don su yabi sunan Ubangiji bisa ga farillan da aka ba wa Isra’ila.


## Zabura 122:5 (id: 10019122005)

5A can kursiyoyin shari’a yake tsaye, kursiyoyin gidan Dawuda.


## Zabura 122:6 (id: 10019122006)

6Ku yi addu’a don salamar Urushalima, “Bari waɗanda suke ƙaunarki su zauna lafiya.


## Zabura 122:7 (id: 10019122007)

7Bari salama ta kasance a katangarki zaman lafiya kuma a fadodinki.”


## Zabura 122:8 (id: 10019122008)

8Saboda ’yan’uwana da kuma abokaina, zan ce, “Salama tă kasance tare da ke.”


## Zabura 122:9 (id: 10019122009)

9Saboda gidan Ubangiji Allahnmu, zan nemi wadatarki.


## Zabura 123:0 (id: 10019123000)

0Waƙar haurawa.


## Zabura 123:1 (id: 10019123001)

## Zabura 123:4 (id: 10019123004)

0Waƙar haurawa. Ta Dawuda.

## Zabura 124:3 (id: 10019124003)

## Zabura 124:4 (id: 10019124004)

## Zabura 124:5 (id: 10019124005)

## Zabura 124:6 (id: 10019124006)

## Zabura 124:7 (id: 10019124007)

## Zabura 125:0 (id: 10019125000)

0Waƙar haurawa.


## Zabura 125:1 (id: 10019125001)

## Zabura 125:2 (id: 10019125002)

0Waƙar haurawa.

## Zabura 127:0 (id: 10019127000)

0Waƙar haurawa. Ta Solomon.


## Zabura 127:1 (id: 10019127001)

1In ba Ubangiji ne ya gina gida ba, masu gininta suna wahala a banza ne. In ba Ubangiji ne yake tsaron birni ba, masu tsaro suna yin tsaro a banza.

## Zabura 137:1 (id: 10019137001)

## Zabura 137:6 (id: 10019137006)

## Zabura 137:8 (id: 10019137008)

## Zabura 137:9 (id: 10019137009)

0Don mai bi da kaɗe\-kaɗe. Ta Dawuda. Zabura ce.

## Zabura 139:3 (id: 10019139003)

## Zabura 139:4 (id: 10019139004)

## Zabura 139:5 (id: 10019139005)

## Zabura 139:6 (id: 10019139006)

## Zabura 139:8 (id: 10019139008)

## Zabura 139:9 (id: 10019139009)

9In na tashi a fikafikan safiya, in na sauka a gefe mai nisa na teku,


## Zabura 139:10 (id: 10019139010)

## Zabura 139:11 (id: 10019139011)

## Zabura 139:12 (id: 10019139012)

## Zabura 139:13 (id: 10019139013)

## Zabura 139:14 (id: 10019139014)

## Zabura 139:20 (id: 10019139020)

## Zabura 139:21 (id: 10019139021)

## Zabura 139:22 (id: 10019139022)

## Zabura 139:24 (id: 10019139024)

## Zabura 145:6 (id: 10019145006)

## Zabura 145:8 (id: 10019145008)

## Zabura 150:5 (id: 10019150005)

## Zabura 150:6 (id: 10019150006)
